x@Neos-MacBook-Pro shahnameh-metaverse-core % cd ~/Desktop/ShahnamehMetaverse/shahnameh-metaverse-core
cat > _extract_econ.js << 'ENDSCRIPT'
const fs = require('fs');
// خواندن فایل
const raw = fs.readFileSync('tweets.js', 'utf8');
// استخراج آرایه JSON از window.YTD.tweets.part0
const jsonStart = raw.indexOf('[');
const jsonEnd = raw.lastIndexOf(']') + 1;
const jsonStr = raw.substring(jsonStart, jsonEnd);
const tweets = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
// کلمات کلیدی مرتبط با اقتصاد، توکن، رنج، وراثت
const keywords = [
  'token', 'coin', 'econom', 'heart', 'breath', 'beat',
  'ran', 'reward', 'distribut', 'supply', 'inherit',
  'generation', 'lineage', 'value', 'price', 'fair',
  'share', 'dividend', 'profit', 'allocation', 'burn',
  'halving', 'vesting', 'genesis', 'presale',
  'preregistration', 'attribution', 'rights', 'royalty',
  'mining', 'mine', 'stake', 'staking', 'airdrop'
];
const matches = tweets.filter(t => {
  const text = (t.tweet.full_text || '').toLowerCase();
  return keywords.some(kw => text.includes(kw));
});
console.log(`Total tweets: ${tweets.length}`);
console.log(`Economic-related tweets: ${matches.length}\n`);
matches.forEach((t, i) => {
  console.log(`--- Tweet #${i+1} | Date: ${t.tweet.created_at} ---`);
  console.log(t.tweet.full_text.slice(0, 300));
  console.log('');
});
// ذخیره در فایل
let out = '';
matches.forEach((t, i) => {
  out += `--- Tweet #${i+1} | Date: ${t.tweet.created_at} ---\n`;
  out += t.tweet.full_text + '\n\n';
});
fs.writeFileSync('_econ_tweets.txt', out);
console.log('Extracted tweets saved to _econ_tweets.txt');
ENDSCRIPT
node _extract_econ.js
Total tweets: 2762
Economic-related tweets: 746

const fs = require('fs');
const raw = fs.readFileSync('tweets.js', 'utf8');
const start = raw.indexOf('[');
const end = raw.lastIndexOf(']') + 1;
const tweets = JSON.parse(raw.substring(start, end));
const kw = ['token','coin','econom','heart','breath','beat','ran','reward','distribut','supply','inherit','generation','lineage','value','price','fair','share','dividend','profit','allocation','burn','halving','vesting','genesis','presale','preregistration','attribution','rights','royalty','mining','stake','airdrop'];
const out = [];
tweets.forEach(t => {
  const txt = t.tweet.full_text || '';
  if (!txt.startsWith('@grok')) return;
  const lc = txt.toLowerCase();
  if (kw.some(k => lc.includes(k))) out.push(txt);
});
console.log('@grok tweets found: ' + out.length);
out.forEach((t,i) => console.log('--- ' + (i+1) + ' ---\n' + t + '\n'));
fs.writeFileSync('_grok_econ.txt', out.join('\n\n---\n\n'));
console.log('Saved to _grok_econ.txt');
